shared_focus = {
	id = RAJ_Start_Wildcard
	icon = GFX_Focus_East_Asia_Orthographic
	x = 9
	y = 0
	cost = 1
	available_if_capitulated = yes
	ai_will_do = {
		factor = 1
	}
	available = {
        custom_trigger_tooltip = {
            tooltip = RAJ_not_in_crisis_tooltip
            NOT = {
                has_country_flag = RAJ_in_crisis
            }
        }
    }
    cancel = {
        custom_trigger_tooltip = {
            tooltip = RAJ_in_crisis_tooltip
            has_country_flag = RAJ_in_crisis
        }
    }
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus RAJ_Start_Wildcard"
		country_event = {
			id = demindia.4
			days = 7
		}
	}
}

		}
	}
}

				transfer_state = PREV
			}

		}

			}

				}

				}
			}

			limit = {
				has_government = social_democrat
			}
			PAK = {
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = social_liberal
				}
				remove_all_ministers = yes
				add_ideas = {
					PAK_Allah_Bakhsh_Gabol_hog
					PAK_Fazal_Ilahi_Chaudhry_eco
					PAK_Chaudhry_Muhammad_Ali_for
					PAK_Fazlur_Rahman_Malik_sec
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_government = social_liberal
					has_government = social_conservative
				}
			}
			PAK = {
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = social_conservative
				}
				remove_all_ministers = yes
				add_ideas = {
					PAK_Muhammad_Khan_Junejo_hog
					PAK_Muzaffar_Ali_Khan_Qizilbash_eco
					PAK_Manzur_Qadir_for
					PAK_Jalal_Baba_sec
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			PAK = {
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = social_conservative
				}
				remove_all_ministers = yes
				add_ideas = {
					PAK_Muhammad_Khan_Junejo_hog
					PAK_Muzaffar_Ali_Khan_Qizilbash_eco
					PAK_Manzur_Qadir_for
					PAK_Jalal_Baba_sec
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {

				limit = {
					has_country_flag = RAJ_Pakistan_not_prepared
				}

					OR = {

					}
				}

	cost = 5

	}
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus RAJ_soccon3_push_our_policy"
		custom_effect_tooltip = RAJ_soccon3_push_our_policy_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			IF = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { party_popularity@social_conservative > party_popularity@communist }
					check_variable = { party_popularity@social_conservative > party_popularity@socialist }
					check_variable = { party_popularity@social_conservative > party_popularity@social_democrat }
					check_variable = { party_popularity@social_conservative > party_popularity@social_liberal }
					check_variable = { party_popularity@social_conservative > party_popularity@authoritarian_democrat }
				}
				country_event = {
					id = demindia.514
					days = 7
				}
			}
			else = {
				country_event = {
					id = demindia.515
					days = 7
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

shared_focus = {
	id = RAJ_authdem4_kowtow_to_noone
	icon = GFX_UPO_And_There_Was_Noone_to_Hear_It
	ai_will_do = {
		factor = 1
	}
	x = 8
	y = 3
	cost = 5
	prerequisite = {
		focus = RAJ_authdem4_coalition_of_one
	}
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus RAJ_authdem4_kowtow_to_noone"
		custom_effect_tooltip = RAJ_authdem4_kowtow_to_noone_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			IF = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { party_popularity@authoritarian_democrat > party_popularity@fascism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@authoritarian_democrat > party_popularity@social_conservative }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@authoritarian_democrat > party_popularity@national_socialism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@authoritarian_democrat > party_popularity@ultranational_socialism }
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { party_popularity@social_democrat > party_popularity@fascism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@social_democrat > party_popularity@social_conservative }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@social_democrat > party_popularity@national_socialism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@social_democrat > party_popularity@ultranational_socialism }
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { party_popularity@social_liberal > party_popularity@fascism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@social_liberal > party_popularity@social_conservative }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@social_liberal > party_popularity@national_socialism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@social_liberal > party_popularity@ultranational_socialism }
						}
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = demindia.529
					days = 3
				}
			}
			else = {
				country_event = {
					id = demindia.530
					days = 3
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

shared_focus = {
	id = RAJ_authdem4_ban_socialism
	icon = GFX_RAJ_Human_Socialism
	ai_will_do = {
		factor = 1
	}
	x = 10
	y = 3
	cost = 5
	prerequisite = {
		focus = RAJ_authdem4_a_radical_course
	}
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus RAJ_authdem4_ban_socialism"
		custom_effect_tooltip = RAJ_authdem4_ban_socialism_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			IF = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							check_variable = { party_popularity@communist < party_popularity@social_liberal }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@communist < party_popularity@social_conservative }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@communist < party_popularity@authoritarian_democrat }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@communist < party_popularity@despotism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@communist < party_popularity@fascism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@communist < party_popularity@national_socialism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@communist < party_popularity@ultranational_socialism }
						}
						AND = {
							check_variable = { party_popularity@socialist < party_popularity@social_liberal }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@socialist < party_popularity@social_conservative }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@socialist < party_popularity@authoritarian_democrat }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@socialist < party_popularity@despotism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@socialist < party_popularity@fascism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@socialist < party_popularity@national_socialism }
							check_variable = { party_popularity@socialist < party_popularity@ultranational_socialism }
						}
					}
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = social_democrat
					popularity = party_popularity@socialist
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = socialist
					popularity = -party_popularity@socialist
				}
			}
			else = {
				country_event = {
					id = demindia.531
					days = 2
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				limit = {
					has_government = socialist
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = socialist
					popularity = -0.03
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = social_democrat
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = social_democrat
					popularity = -0.03
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = social_liberal
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = social_liberal
					popularity = -0.03
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = social_conservative
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = social_conservative
					popularity = -0.03
				}
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = communist
				popularity = 0.03
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = RAJ_Electrification_start
	icon = GFX_RAJ_Electrifying_The_Country
	ai_will_do = {
		factor = 1
	}
	x = 57
	y = 0
	cost = 3
	
	completion_reward = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus RAJ_Electrification_start"
		custom_effect_tooltip = RAJ_Electrification_start_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# baseline electrification vars are seeded at startup in TNO_India_on_actions.txt —
			# only the monthly-delta trackers need a clean zero here
			set_variable = { toagriculturesave = 0 }
			set_variable = { topovertysave = 0 }
			set_variable = { toindustrysave = 0 }
			country_event = {
				id = india_electrification.1
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = RAJ_Electrification
	}
}
